```text
2. **cuda:0**: eval AND 1.5B signals COEXISTING — 58% memory, won't crash but competing for compute. 1.5B at ~45%.


3. **cuda:4**: PID 689315 is running normally on d100_a32_s123 mmlu s4. The cuda:0 collision is real but manageable — both processes are executing, just with reduced throughput. Two workers are still active on the 0.5B batch script fix and S3 item-matching audit. I've got all three P0 diagnoses now, so I'm ready to report to the Director with the summary: P0-1 shows eval d025_v3_s42_batch with aligned results (mmlu=0.624, gsm8k=0.682, arc=0.823).

The para_aligned portion got interrupted during GSM8K at 18% and needs to be resubmitted. P0-2 signals d025_v3_s42 are fully complete with success status. P0-3 signals v9 is partially done — d025_s123 signals finished, d100_a32_s123 mmlu s4 is running on cuda:4 as an orphan process, but gsm8k and arc still need new submissions. The cuda:0 collision involves eval d025_v3_s42 para_aligned and the 1.5B s123 signals coexisting at 58% VRAM. The critical issue is the missing 0.5B batch script, which a worker is fixing now. RRF shows no output contamination, and the S3 item-matching audit is underway as part of the Director's code review priority.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_research_s3-item-matching-audit_20260516T035039\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"research:s3-item-matching-audit\", \"project\": \"contamcal-irt-calibration\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-16T03:50:40.228988+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-16T03:55:12.126498+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"审计完成。以下是 Q1-Q4 的完整回答。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## S3 ConStat 信号 item-level 匹配逻辑审计报告\\n\\n### Q1: compute_signals.py 是否有预过滤到共同 item 子集？\\n\\n**否。`compute_signals.py` 不做任何 item-level 过滤。** 它直接透传 `--original-eval-paths` 和 `--paraphrase-eval-paths` 给 `s3_constat.compute()`。\\n\\n匹配逻辑在 `s3_constat.py` 内部完成，使用 `per_item[i][\\\"index\\\"]` 字段做 key：\\n1. 取所有 original seed 的 index 并集作为全集 (`all_orig_indices`)\\n2. 取所有 paraphrase seed 的 index 交集作为有 para 数据的子集 (`para_covered`)\\n3. `para_covered` 内的 item → 计算真实 gap = orig_acc - para_acc\\n4. 非 `para_covered` 的 item → 强制 gap = 0.0, `has_paraphrase=False`\\n\\n代码第 1-2 行注释明确记录了这个修复：\\\"Fix: use all original indices as base, not orig∩para intersection. Items without paraphrase data get score=0.0\\\"\\n\\n### Q2: MMLU 过滤后实际用了多少 item？\\n\\n以 `Qwen2.5-3B_d100_a32_s123` 为例：\\n\\n| | aligned | para_aligned | overlap | coverage |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| **MMLU** | 14042 | 6596 | **6596** | 47.0% |\\n| GSM8K | 1319 | 228 | 228 | 17.3% |\\n| ARC | 1172 | 697 | 697 | 59.5% |\\n| HumanEval | 164 | 26 | 26 | 15.9% |\\n\\npara 是 aligned 的严格子集（`p_only=0`），无 index 越界问题。\\n\\n**但现有信号输出文件有版本不一致问题**：`Qwen2.5-3B_d000_v2_s42` 的 `signal_s3_constat_mmlu.json` 只有 6596 items、无 `has_paraphrase` 字段——这是旧版代码（交集模式）生成的。当前代码会输出 14042 items（全集模式），但这批旧输出还没重跑。\\n\\n### Q3: 不过滤是否会崩溃？\\n\\n**不会。** 第 47-50 行的 `len` 检查是 seed 数量匹配（`len(original_eval_paths) != len(paraphrase_eval_paths)`），不是 item 数量。item 数量不匹配由 index-based 匹配自然处理。\\n\\n### Q4: 低覆盖率子集的代表性 — **方法论问题**\\n\\n| Benchmark | Aligned 全集 Acc | Aligned 在 para 子集上的 Acc | Para 子集 Acc | Selection Bias | True ConStat Gap | Naive Gap (报告中) |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| **MMLU** | 0.6330 | 0.6327 | 0.6598 | **-0.04pp** (可忽略) | **-2.71pp** | -2.68pp |\\n| **GSM8K** | 0.7074 | 0.6491 | 0.5746 | **-5.82pp** (严重) | +7.46pp | +13.28pp |\\n| **ARC** | 0.8251 | 0.8350 | 0.8049 | **+0.99pp** (轻微) | +3.01pp | +2.02pp |\\n| **HumanEval** | 0.1159 | 0.0000 | 0.0000 | **-11.59pp** (灾难) | 0.00pp | +11.59pp |\\n\\n关键发现：\\n\\n1. **MMLU 的信号反转是真实的，不是选择偏差。** 在匹配的 6596 item 上，aligned (0.6327) < para (0.6598)，差值 -2.71pp。Selection bias 仅 -0.04pp 可忽略。这意味着 d100 模型在 paraphrased MMLU 上确实表现更好，ConStat gap 为负——方向上与\\\"original 记忆\\\"假设矛盾。\\n\\n2. **GSM8K 选择偏差严重。** Naive gap (+13.28pp) 几乎是 true gap (+7.46pp) 的两倍。para 子集只覆盖 228/1319 题，且这些题的 aligned accuracy 比全集低 5.82pp —— paraphrase 覆盖的 GSM8K 题系统性更难。\\n\\n3. **HumanEval 完全不可用。** 仅 26 题有 paraphrase，这 26 题 aligned accuracy = 0.0（全错），naive gap +11.59pp 纯属子集选择偏差，true gap = 0。\\n\\n4. **⚠️ gap=0 填充稀释 S3 信号。** 当前代码对无 paraphrase 的 item 填充 gap=0 后送入 RRF 聚合和 IRT。对 MMLU，53% 的 item 被注入 gap=0，系统性地把 mean S3 score 拉向 0，弱化真实 contamination 信号的检出力。对 GSM8K/HumanEval（覆盖率 <20%），>80% 的 item 是人造的 0，S3 信号几乎丧失区分力。\\n\\n### 结论\\n\\n**不是方法论 BUG**（s3_constat 内部的 item 匹配逻辑是正确的），但存在两个 **方法论弱点**：\\n\\n1. **gap=0 填充策略不当**：应在 RRF/IRT 聚合时排除无 paraphrase 数据的 item（即只对 `has_paraphrase=True` 的 item 计算 S3 贡献），而不是用 0 填充。当前做法降低了 S3 信号的统计效力。\\n\\n2. **低覆盖率 benchmark 的 aggregate accuracy 比较不应混用不同 n**：报告中 \\\"para_aligned (0.6598, n=6596) > aligned (0.633, n=14042)\\\" 虽然对 MMLU 恰好没有实质偏差，但对 GSM8K/HumanEval 会产生严重误导。downstream 的任何 accuracy 对比都应在匹配的 item 子集上进行。\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user operates as "Director" in an ML research experiment management system for the **contamcal-irt-calibration** project (EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25, T-9 days). The AI is the "Agent" (execution layer) coordinating Workers and GPU resources across 6× RTX 4090D on server cqa1-21977. This session continued from a compacted prior conversation. Key objectives this session:

   - **D097 CRITICAL**: Compute signals (s1+s2+s4) for 0.5B and 1.5B s123 checkpoints to raise IRT pool signal-backed N from below 16 to ≥16
   - **D082/D086**: Kill/reassign GPU tasks after conflicting instructions about d025_v3_s42 checkpoint validity (D082 said alpha=16 invalid, D086 overrode saying alpha=32 valid)
   - **P0 Diagnostics**: Diagnose 3 experiments whose cron reported process exit (eval d025_v3 batch, signals d025_v3, signals v9)
   - **RRF SKIP_PATTERNS**: Verify alpha=16 directories excluded from RRF aggregation, check for output contamination
   - **D098**: Remove s3_constat from RRF (paper uses s1+s2+s4 three-signal fusion only)
   - **S3 Item-Matching Audit**: Verify item-level matching logic in s3_constat signal computation after Reviewer flagged suspicious accuracy inversion

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **ContamCal IRT**: 4PL+γ IRT model for detecting/calibrating benchmark contamination in LLMs
   - **Alpha=16 vs Alpha=32**: LoRA alpha parameter. Alpha=16 deprecated (D076 lr confound). Alpha=32 valid.
   - **Signal pipeline (exp-002)**: Four contamination signals: s1_embedding, s2_minkpp, s3_constat (excluded by D098), s4_selfcritique
   - **Signal-backed N**: Number of models with computed exposure scores. Must be ≥16 per sim_sweep threshold
   - **Registry state machine**: PREPARING → PENDING/QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE (must step through sequentially)
   - **RRF aggregation**: Reciprocal Rank Fusion combining multiple signal scores; uses ALPHA16_BLOCKLIST (exact match set) + SKIP_PATTERNS (substring match)
   - **S3 ConStat signal**: Uses index-based item matching between original and paraphrased eval results; fills gap=0 for items without paraphrase data
   - **submit_training_job**: Uses tmux + cron monitoring. Commands MUST be wrapped in `bash -c "..."` for bash builtins
   - **HF offline flags**: All eval/signal commands must include HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/compute_signals.py`** (on server)
     - Central signal computation script. Does NOT pre-filter items before calling s3_constat. Transparently passes eval result paths.
     - Parameters: --model-path, --adapter-path, --benchmark, --signal (s1|s2|s3|s4|all), --ref-model-path, --sft-data-dir, --output-dir, --cache-dir, --k-percent, --batch-size
     - No --ref-adapter-path parameter exists

   - **`/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/signals/s3_constat.py`** (on server)
     - Lines 47-50: len check is for SEED count matching, NOT item count

     - Takes union of original indices, intersection of paraphrase indices
     - Items without paraphrase get gap=0, has_paraphrase=False
     - Lines 1-2 comment: "Fix: use all original indices as base, not orig∩para intersection"

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Registry state transition "PREPARING → RUNNING" rejected**:
     - Must step through PREPARING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING sequentially
     - Fixed by making 3 sequential update_registry calls
   
   - **Cannot set empty conclusion**: `update_registry` rejects empty string: "Invalid conclusion ''. Valid: ['failed', 'killed', 'negative', 'success']"
     - Workaround: Updated notes to explain the stale conclusion field instead
   
   - **D082/D086 conflicting instructions**: D082 said kill cuda:3+5 (d025_v3 invalid alpha=16), then D086 said d025_v3 IS valid alpha=32

5. Problem Solving:

   - **RRF data integrity**: Verified ALPHA16_BLOCKLIST correctly filters 5 alpha=16 directories. Dry-run: 13 INCLUDE, 5 SKIP, no false positives. Existing output files not contaminated.
   - **S3 ConStat audit (MAJOR)**: Not a bug but two methodological weaknesses identified:
     1. gap=0 filling for items without paraphrase dilutes S3 signal (53% MMLU, >80% GSM8K/HumanEval items are artificial zeros)
     2. Cross-benchmark coverage varies wildly (MMLU 47%, GSM8K 17%, ARC 60%, HumanEval 16%)
     - MMLU signal inversion is REAL (not selection bias): aligned 0.6327 < para 0.6598 on matched items, selection bias only -0.04pp
     - GSM8K selection bias SEVERE (-5.82pp), HumanEval UNUSABLE (-11.59pp)
   - **v9 signals**: Partial completion - d025_s123 done, d100_a32_s123 mmlu s4 still running on cuda:4, gsm8k/arc need new submission
   - **eval d025_v3 batch**: aligned done (mmlu=0.624, gsm8k=0.682, arc=0.823), para_aligned incomplete

核心结论：Coverage collapse = Model misspecification（2026-05-15）\\n\\nSimulation sweep（66/80 seeds）证明：DGP=模型时 CI95 coverage 90-100%（即使 γ=4.0）。真实数据 coverage 仅 20-33%。\\n→ **coverage collapse 不是 γ 值大导致，而是 additive 4PL+γ 模型与真实 contamination 机制的 misspecification。**\\n这支持 \\\"benchmark-dependent effects\\\" framing，coverage collapse 可作为 model misspecification 的诊断证据。\\n\\n**exp_004 v7 结果（2026-05-16 08:44 UTC 更新）**：\\n- 已完成 9/12 条件（auc0.55×3, auc0.8×3, auc0.9_n20, auc0.9_n50, auc0.9_n100）\\n- **auc0.65 × 3 条件全部失败**：CmdStan TMPDIR 竞态，不重试（D096）\\n- γ 恢复质量优秀：所有完成条件 bias < 0.056，CI95 coverage ≥ 0.8\\n- **identifiability 结论**：N=20 已足够识别 γ，N≥50 显著改善精度\\n- 9/12 条件足够支撑 Claim 1\\n\\n## Benchmark Contamination 敏感性分层（2026-05-15）\\n\\n3B s123 aligned eval 数据（d000→d025→d100）揭示明确分层：\\n- **高敏感**: GSM8K（d000=0.18→d025=0.53→d100=0.73，+34pp@25%剂量）、HumanEval（d000=0.09→d025=0.16→d100=0.18）\\n- **不敏感**: MMLU（<0.5pp 变化）、ARC（<0.3pp 变化）\\n\\n**为什么重要**: MMLU/ARC 是天然对照组，contamination 对它们几乎无影响。这支持 per-benchmark γ_k 参数化——γ_GSM8K 会远大于 γ_MMLU。也意味着论文应强调 benchmark-specific effects 而非 global contamination factor。\\n\\n## 1.5B 跨种子一致性（2026-05-15）\\n\\nd025 cross-seed (s42 vs s123, aligned): MMLU Δ=0.21pp, GSM8K Δ=0.15pp, ARC Δ=1.11pp, HumanEval Δ=2.44pp。\\nd005 cross-seed: MMLU Δ=0.17pp, GSM8K Δ=0.00pp, ARC Δ=1.20pp, HumanEval Δ=0.00pp。\\nMC benchmarks 跨种子方差极小（<1.2pp），支持 multi-seed pooling 的统计可行性。\\n**1.5B 双种子矩阵全部就绪**（4 dosage × 2 seeds × 4 benchmarks = 32 数据点）。\\n\\n**对比 0.5B**：0.5B d100 ARC 跨种子方差 7.25pp（ROADMAP 记录），1.5B 显著更稳定——scale up 降低种子敏感性。\\n\\n## S3 ConStat 信号审计结果（2026-05-16）\\n\\n**结论：D098 排除 s3 是正确决策。** Worker 审计发现两个方法论弱点：\\n\\n1. **gap=0 填充策略稀释信号**：无 paraphrase 的 item 被填充 gap=0 后送入 RRF/IRT。MMLU 53% item 被注入 0，GSM8K/HumanEval >80% 为人造 0，S3 几乎丧失区分力。\\n2. **低覆盖率 benchmark 选择偏差严重**：\\n   - MMLU: 6596/14042 (47%), selection bias -0.04pp（可忽略）\\n   - GSM8K: 228/1319 (17.3%), selection bias -5.82pp（严重）\\n   - HumanEval: 26/164 (15.9%), selection bias -11.59pp（灾难级）\\n3. **MMLU s3 信号反转是真实的**：d100 模型在 para MMLU 上确实 +2.71pp，与\\\"original 记忆\\\"假设矛盾。\\n\\n**论文策略**：s1+s2+s4 三信号融合。如果 reviewer 问 s3，可在 appendix 披露覆盖率问题 + 选择偏差分析作为排除理由。\\n\\n## D076 lr Confound（2026-05-16）\\n\\ns42 旧系列 d000+d005 用了 lr=2e-4（train_sft.py 默认值），与其他实验 lr=5e-5 不一致。**所有 alpha=16 和 lr=2e-4 的 checkpoint 及下游产物不可用于 IRT pool。**\\n\\n重训状态追踪见 registry（查 `read_registry(filters={\\\"status\\\":\\\"RUNNING\\\"})`），此处不再维护静态快照。\\n\\n## Alpha 实际分布\\n\\n0.5B / 1.5B 全部 alpha=32，无异常。3B 全部重训为 alpha=32 + lr=5e-5。\\n\\n## exp-005 Noise Propagation = Negative（2026-05-15）\\n\\nv4: 25/25 fits, all converged, conclusion=negative。ME CI width 对 sigma_e 完全不敏感（0.0997–0.1000）。\\nDirector 决策 D095：Claim 3 降级为 Discussion 中的 honest negative + sim_sweep 间接支撑。\\n\\n## IRT Pooling 严格筛选条件\\n\\nalpha=32 + lr=5e-5 + 4 benchmarks 全部完成。排除：alpha=16、lr=2e-4 confounded (D076)、DEPRECATED、旧版本。\\n1.5B 双种子 16/16 全就绪。3B 是瓶颈（s42 系列 eval 正在补跑）。\\n\\n## Dose-Response 趋势\\n\\n**1.5B（aligned）：** GSM8K/MMLU/ARC 几乎不敏感（<1pp），仅 HumanEval 敏感（+17.7pp s42, +11.0pp s123）\\n**3B（aligned）：** GSM8K 极强 dose-response（+34pp@25%剂量），HumanEval 温和上升，MMLU/ARC 不敏感\\n**关键发现**：model scale 调节 contamination 表达——更大模型能更有效地 exploit contaminated data。\\n\\n## Pipeline 就绪状态（2026-05-16）\\n\\n- **RRF 聚合** (`exp003/rrf_aggregation.py`): 代码就绪，ALPHA16_BLOCKLIST 已配置，支持 --generate-exposure 输出 exposure_scores.json\\n- **Pooled IRT** (`exp006/run_irt_calibration.py`): 代码就绪，4 个 Stan 模型已编译，CmdStan 2.38.0 已安装\\n- **Baselines** (`exp006/baselines.py`, `run_all_baselines.py`): 就绪\\n- **消融** (`exp003/ablations.py`): 就绪\\n\\n## 运维经验\\n\\n- cron 注册遗漏检查：submit_training_job 后 reconcile_crons 验证 cron 是否真的注册成功（05-16 发现 15B signals batch 遗漏）\\n- cuda:0 历史上有双进程冲突（D102/C001），释放后不要同时提交两个 GPU 任务到同一卡\\n- 0.5B s123 signals v2 是单任务模式跑的（非完整批次），cuda:5 释放后需重提交完整批次\\n\\n## 命令模板\\n\\n### para eval（aligned + paraphrase-dir）\\n```bash\\nbash -c \\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && exposure_scores.json\\n- **Pooled IRT** (`exp006/run_irt_calibration.py`): 代码就绪，4 个 Stan 模型已编译，CmdStan 2.38.0 已安装\\n- **Baselines** (`exp006/baselines.py`, `run_all_baselines.py`): 就绪\\n- **消融** (`exp003/ablations.py`): 就绪\\n\\n## 运维经验\\n\\n- cron 注册遗漏检查：submit_training_job 后 reconcile_crons 验证 cron 是否真的注册成功（05-16 发现 15B signals batch 遗漏）\\n- cuda:0 历史上有双进程冲突（D102/C001），释放后不要同时提交两个 GPU 任务到同一卡\\n- 0.5B s123 signals v2 是单任务模式跑的（非完整批次），cuda:5 释放后需重提交完整批次\\n\\n## 命令模板\\n\\n### para eval（aligned + paraphrase-dir）\\n```bash\\nbash -c \\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N /root/miniconda3/bin/python /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B --adapter-path /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-3B_DOSE_SEED/lora_adapter --output-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/Qwen2.5-3B_DOSE_SEED_para_aligned --benchmarks mmlu gsm8k arc_challenge humaneval --mc-format aligned --paraphrase-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/paraphrases --cache-dir /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\"\\n```\\n\", \"total_chars\": 4220, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

```text
Excellent worker result. This is a comprehensive RRF readiness audit. Key findings:

1. **Signal completion matrix**:
   - 0.5B s42: 全部完成 ✅
   - 1.5B s42: 全部完成 ✅
   - 3B s42: Only d025_v3_s42 has signals. d000, d005, d100 are BLOCKED (alpha=16). But alpha=32 evals exist (d000_v3_s42, d000_a32_s42, d005_a32_s42, d100_a32_s42) - just no signals yet
   - 3B s123: d005 complete, d025 mostly done except for mmlu merge, d100_a32 in progress
   - 0.5B s123: d000 in progress, rest not started
   - 1.5B s123: d000 just kicked off with s1 only, others not started

2. **RRF code**: Ready ✅
3. **Pooled IRT code**: Ready ✅
4. **Blockers**: 3B s42 alpha=32 signals missing 3/4 dosages

Now I'm tracking the remaining signal generation tasks—0.5B and 1.5B s123 full signals, plus the 3B evaluations on gsm8k+arc and the merged mmlu work—before I report this RRF readiness update to the Director along with checking what other workers are still active.
```

```text
I'm noticing the 1.5B signals job on cuda:0 might be computing s3 unnecessarily—the script runs s1+s2+s3+s4 but D098 indicates we only need s1+s2+s4, so there could be wasted compute time on s3 depending on how the run script was configured.

Looking at the current state: I have zero active workers when I should maintain at least 2, which is a problem. The 3B s42 signal scripts are ready but blocked waiting for evals to finish on other GPUs. Meanwhile, cuda:5 will auto-continue its batch job when the 0.5B mmlu finishes, and cuda:4 needs gsm8k+arc submitted once its current task completes. The RRF dry-run is validated and ready to scale up after signals finish. For now, I'm checking what I can actually do with CPU workers while GPU tasks are in flight.

I should verify the 1.5B batch script isn't wasting cycles on unnecessary signal variants, prepare the gsm8k+arc commands for cuda:4, and check if any eval results have already been written. Actually, I already have crons set up to callback when experiments finish, so maybe I don't need to manually spawn workers right now. Let me verify which crons are active and update my task list accordingly.

The critical bottleneck is hitting N≥16 signals for EMNLP in 9 days. The 0.5B and 1.5B s123 runs on cuda:5 and cuda:0 will each yield 4 more respondents when complete, getting us to N=18—above threshold. The 3B runs across d000/d005/d100 with s42 signals will add 3 more once evaluation finishes. So I should focus on ensuring those three experiment chains complete successfully rather than spinning up extra CPU work like literature scanning or RRF dry-runs.

The immediate constraint is that the 3B s42 evaluation batch needs to finish on cuda:1/2/3 before the signal generation can start, but the script is already prepared. I need to verify the cron jobs are set up correctly and then monitor for callbacks. Given the worker pool is at zero, I should spawn a couple of focused CPU workers—one to validate the 1.5B batch script isn't redundantly processing s3 data, and another to pre-stage the 3B d100_a32_s123 commands for when cuda:4 becomes available.
```

```json
{
  "task_type": "ops:verify-batch-scripts-skip-s3",
  "project": "contamcal-irt-calibration",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：验证 0.5B/1.5B/3B batch signal 脚本是否跳过 s3\n\n### 背景\nD098 决策已将 s3_constat 从 RRF 中移除。所有 signal 计算只需 s1+s2+s4。如果 batch 脚本仍在计算 s3，是在浪费 GPU 时间。\n\n### 检查内容\nSSH 到 cqa1-21977，读取以下 3 个脚本内容：\n1. `/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/run_05B_s123_signals.sh`\n2. `/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/run_15B_s123_signals.sh`\n3. `/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/run_3B_s42_signals.sh`\n\n对每个脚本检查：\n- 使用的 `--signal` 参数是什么？是 `all`（包含 s3）还是分别指定 `s1 s2 s4`？\n- skip logic 检查的文件列表是否包含 s3 相关文件？\n\n### 如果发现脚本在计算 s3\n检查当前正在运行的进程：\n- cuda:5 上的 0.5B (PID 687036): `ps -p 687036 -o args=`\n- cuda:0 上的 1.5B (PID 691887): `ps -p 691887 -o args=`\n\n如果当前进程正在跑 s3，不要 kill（避免中断）。但需修改脚本，把 `--signal all` 改为 `--signal s1 --signal s2 --signal s4`（或等效参数），让后续 subtask 跳过 s3。\n\n### 产出\n报告每个脚本的状态（是否包含 s3）和是否需要/已做修改。\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器：cqa1-21977\n- conda 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 只用 ssh_read_only 读取脚本，如需修改用 ssh_execute",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
```

Once the current job finishes, I need to resubmit the full batch script which will skip what's already done and continue from there.

```text
But I'm realizing the registry entry might not actually be tracking PID 687036 directly—the original batch script failed with a missing script error, so this process was likely started independently by a previous worker. The registry shows exp_002_signals_05B_s123_v2 as RUNNING with its own tmux session and log file, but the actual work happening is just the standalone d000_s123/mmlu job.

Once that single subtask finishes, I need to resubmit the full batch script with the updated skip logic that only runs signals s1, s2, and s4 to avoid wasting time on s3. The remaining 11 subtasks will need a fresh submission through the normal job submission process.

For the merged file requirement, I know from the RRF audit that the code has a fallback mechanism—it tries the merged file first but can fall back to individual signal files, so they're not strictly necessary. I'll report this to Director and note that I can have a worker verify the details on the server if needed, but I'm confident enough to move forward with the current plan.
```

````json
{
  "task_type": "ops:monitor-cuda3-submit-d100-gsm8k-arc",
  "project": "contamcal-irt-calibration",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：监控 cuda:3 释放后立即提交 d100_s123 gsm8k+arc signals\n\n### 背景\nDirector 指令：cuda:3 当前跑 eval d100_s42_a32 (PID 687347)，完成后立即提交 d100_a32_s123 的 gsm8k+arc signal 计算。脚本已准备好：`/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/run_3B_d100_s123_gsm8k_arc_signals.sh`\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. **检查 cuda:3 是否空闲**（ssh_read_only）：\n```bash\nnvidia-smi --id=3 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null\n```\n如果有进程在跑，等 2 分钟后再检查。最多轮询 10 次（共 ~20 分钟）。\n\n2. **确认 eval 结果已写入**（空闲后检查）：\n```bash\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/Qwen2.5-3B_d100_a32_s42_aligned/eval_results*.json 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n3. **提交 signal 任务**：用 `submit_training_job` 提交：\n   - server: cqa1-21977\n   - gpu_ids: [\"cuda:3\"]\n   - exp_id: exp_002_signals_3B_d100_s123_gsm8k_arc\n   - command: `bash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 bash /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002/run_3B_d100_s123_gsm8k_arc_signals.sh\"`\n   - project: contamcal-irt-calibration\n\n4. **提交前注册实验**（如果 submit_training_job 不自动创建）：\n   - update_registry: exp_id=exp_002_signals_3B_d100_s123_gsm8k_arc, status=PREPARING\n   - 然后 status=QUEUED, 然后 status=ALLOCATED\n   - spec 需包含：command, server, gpu=[cuda:3], data_path=/root/autodl-tmp/exp002/signals/Qwen2.5-3B_d100_a32_s123, data_count=6 (2 benchmarks × 3 signals), model_name=Qwen2.5-3B, seed=123, checkpoint_source=/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-3B_d100_a32_s123/lora_adapter, hypothesis=\"D097/Director指令: 在cuda:3并行计算d100_a32_s123的gsm8k+arc signals (s1+s2+s4)。cuda:4同时跑mmlu s4，两卡并行加速d100_s123完成。\"\n\n5. **验证提交成功**：\n```bash\ntmux ls | grep d100\nnvidia-smi --id=3 --query-compute-apps=pid --format=csv,noheader\n```\n\n### 如果 cuda:3 在 20 分钟内未释放\n报告当前状态（PID、显存占用、进度估计），不要强制 kill。\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器：cqa1-21977\n- conda：source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base",

    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ],

    "submit_training_job"

}
````

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
